The build made 1978 commits:

| Commits | Kind |
| ---: | --- |
| 235 | first version of an act |
| 1261 | one law on its in-force day |
| 482 | changes no amendment note explains, credited to the publisher |

Other counts:
- 0 commits on the branch `futuro`: laws not yet in force on 2026-10-31.
- 2 commits dated 1 January 1970: in force before 1970, with the real day in their `In-force` trailer.
- 2218 laws in the record.
- 3383 tags.

## Dates

Each commit is dated at noon (America/Mexico_City) on the day the change took effect. The other dates, enacted and published, are trailers.

When a law gives no date of its own, the default rule applies: Código Civil Federal art. 3: laws bind and take effect three days after publication in the Diario Oficial de la Federación; art. 4: a law that fixes its own start day binds from that day https://www.diputados.gob.mx/LeyesBiblio/pdf/2_110121.pdf

## How the history is built

`tools/build.py` turns the corpus into this history in one pass: one commit per law, on the day it took effect. The same corpus always gives the same commit hashes. `tools/check_repo.py` reads every version back from git and compares it with the corpus. `tools/on.py` shows the law as it stood on any date. The corpus format is described in `tools/SCHEMA.md`.

- Corpus: sha256 `8c0171481eee86a593cf207cb111736c7f87fdedeb886f1ff8dc84cfc8aa3f65` (89686525 bytes).
- Builder blob hashes (`git hash-object`): `build.py` `a4faa25af85facb64b956b372f6b528aafdd7ec1`, `check.py` `3d00541d258745461ab52b1b0cb4e1716a27a95d`, `on.py` `644a6c0857fbcc72562032b58d57aa113fee0641`.
- Python 3.12.10; git version 2.46.0.windows.1.

## Checks

2053 versions of 235 acts read back from git on the day each took effect and on the day before: 0 mismatches. Trailers: 0 commits whose trailers git cannot parse. Health: 0 merge commits on main, 0 commit dates that go backwards, 0 commits dated after the build.

A second build from the same corpus into an empty folder gave identical hashes for all 1978 history commits and 3383 tags (history tip `d55ca2d13f7df66655888ef6c0c81ccdbbc8f67d`).

## Decisions made by hand

- Constitución: history starts at the oldest consolidated copy the Internet Archive holds (Cámara de Diputados pdf/1.pdf captured 20060813201302, last reform DOF 2006-04-07); 1917-2006 is not rebuilt, and the first version names the 170 earlier decrees.
- Constitución: 8 archived copies cut off at exactly 1 MiB were dropped (20190923002305, 20200918083844, 20210125021153, 20210927164804, 20220516151601, 20230608153526, 20230925125132, 20241203191611); for each last-reform date the newest copy is used (78 copies from 90 readable ones).
- Constitución: in-force dates come from each decree's first transitorio that speaks of entry into force ("al día siguiente de su publicación" = DOF + 1, "el día de su publicación" = DOF, a fixed date, or "a los N días"); otherwise Código Civil Federal art. 3 (three days after publication). Counts: Código Civil Federal art. 3 (tres días después de su publicación): 1; a los 120 días de su publicación: 1; día de su publicación: 2; día siguiente al de su publicación: 116.
- Constitución: where a decree puts single provisions in force on later dates (for example the 2014 political-electoral reform, DOF 10-02-2014), the whole article takes effect on the decree's general date; the staggered dates are in the decree's transitorios unit.
- Constitución: credits come from the publisher's paragraph notes ("Párrafo reformado DOF dd-mm-yyyy", in copies from 2013 on, read for every unit across all later copies): a note date inside the gap between two copies names the decree published that day. Several decrees that day, or no note date: the decree whose own title names the article, only if exactly one fits. Otherwise the change is unexplained and dated on the copy's last-reform day. Counts: {'credited_notes': 413, 'credited_title': 0, 'credited_self': 120, 'unexplained': 142}.
- Constitución: units are the preámbulo, the headings (estructura), articles 1-136, the 1917 transitorios and one unit per reform decree's transitorios as the publisher prints them; the publisher's notes and cover page are left out of the text.
- Constitución: copies before 2013 print the transitorios of only some earlier decrees; transitorios the publisher added later for decrees before the gap arrive as unexplained publisher changes.
- Constitución: a copy prints a decree's wording from its publication day, but each change here takes effect only on its decree's in-force day, so no wording appears before its decree applies.
- Constitución: errata ("Fe de erratas") that the DOF printed and the publisher reproduces with the decrees are laws of their own, dated by Código Civil Federal art. 3; one (DOF 25-06-2009) corrects art. 16.
- Constitución: when two decrees were published on the same day and a note names that day, the change is credited only if exactly one decree's title fits the article (a title listing other articles does not fit; "diversas disposiciones" fits any); otherwise it is unexplained, as for art. 41 on DOF 02-06-2026.

- LOPJF: the law of 2024 (DOF 20-12-2024) abrogated the law of the same name published on 7 June 2021 (its transitorio Segundo), and took over its file name. Copies are told apart by their own cover: those printing "Nueva Ley ... 7 de junio de 2021" (captures to February 2024) are the 2021 law and are left out; the record starts with the 2024 law as enacted. The 2021 law (and the law of 1995 before it) is not in the record.

- LAASSP: credits come from the publisher's paragraph notes ("Párrafo reformado DOF dd-mm-yyyy"), read for every unit across all later copies: a note date between two copies names the publication the reform page lists for that day, only when exactly one does; a reform decree's own transitorios are credited to it. Otherwise the change is unexplained (the publisher's, often its own proofreading) and takes effect once every publication between the two copies is in force. ## What AI did and did not do

The scripts that read the sources and build this repository were written by Claude Code running Claude Opus 5.5 (`claude-opus-5-5`) under Lexifina's direction.

No model wrote, translated or corrected legal text. No model runs at build time. Every word of law in this repository comes from the official sources above.

The commit messages are written by `build.py` from the source data.

Model use at data-build time:
- Claude Code (Claude Opus 5.5, claude-opus-5-5) wrote Lexifina's code. No model output is used as legal text, dates or credits: all come from the official copies by deterministic code.
